{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgNAERCdSoGezDvV1WVCk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019cGkDzgca71fQNTHkQLTei","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261011-092100/floydwarshall_claude-fable-5.1-cc-medium_hybrid_r3/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp","content":"// Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\n//\n// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation.\n//\n//  * MPI    : the distance matrix is distributed by contiguous blocks of rows\n//             (64-row block granularity) across ranks; every rank drives one\n//             GPU. Each block-round of the blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm\n//             requires one broadcast of the pivot row panel (64 x n) from the\n//             rank that owns it.\n//  * CUDA   : all O(n^3) work is done on the GPU with the classic three-phase\n//             blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm (diagonal tile, pivot row/column\n//             tiles, remaining tiles) using shared-memory tiling and register\n//             blocking.\n//  * OpenMP : host-side O(n^2) work (matrix initialisation / packing, result\n//             gathering, validation) runs multithreaded on each rank.\n//\n// The computed distance matrix is bit-identical to the sequential algorithm\n// (the blocked formulation computes exactly the same shortest-path values).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Blocking parameters for the GPU kernels\nconstexpr int TILE = 64;             // tile edge length (nodes)\nconstexpr int TX = 64;               // threads in x (one per tile column)\nconstexpr int TY = 16;               // threads in y\nconstexpr int RPT = TILE / TY;       // rows per thread (register blocking)\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                              \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                   \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                   \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err__),    \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                              \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                             \\\n        }                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                           \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                                   \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %d at %s:%d\\n\", err__, __FILE__, __LINE__);    \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                             \\\n        }                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n//\n// All matrices on the device are row-major with leading dimension `ld`\n// (= padded number of nodes). Tile (bi, bj) starts at (bi*TILE)*ld + bj*TILE.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Phase 1: the pivot diagonal tile, fully dependent -> one thread block.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase1Kernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n               const size_t ld, const int kb, const size_t tileRowOffset) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int s[TILE][TILE];\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + (tileRowOffset) * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + (tileRowOffset) * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        s[ty + r * TY][tx] = d[(size_t)(ty + r * TY) * ld + tx];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) { pk[r] = 0; changed[r] = false; }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int skj = s[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const int i = ty + r * TY;\n            const unsigned int v = s[i][k] + skj;\n            if (v < s[i][tx]) {\n                s[i][tx] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2 (row): tiles (kb, j) for all j != kb, using the finished diagonal tile.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase2RowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                  const size_t ld, const int kb, const size_t tileRowOffset) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int diag[TILE][TILE];\n    __shared__ unsigned int cur[TILE][TILE];\n\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x;\n    if (bj == kb) return;\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const unsigned int* dg = dist + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        diag[i][tx] = dg[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        cur[i][tx] = d[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) { pk[r] = 0; changed[r] = false; }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int ckj = cur[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const int i = ty + r * TY;\n            const unsigned int v = diag[i][k] + ckj;\n            if (v < cur[i][tx]) {\n                cur[i][tx] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = cur[i][tx];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2 (column): local tiles (i, kb) for all local i != kb-tile, using the\n// finished diagonal tile (taken from the broadcast pivot panel).\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase2ColKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                  const unsigned int* __restrict__ panel, const size_t ld,\n                  const int kb, const int kbLocal) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int diag[TILE][TILE];\n    __shared__ unsigned int cur[TILE][TILE];\n\n    const int bi = blockIdx.x;\n    if (bi == kbLocal) return;\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const unsigned int* dg = panel + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + (size_t)bi * TILE * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + (size_t)bi * TILE * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        diag[i][tx] = dg[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        cur[i][tx] = d[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) { pk[r] = 0; changed[r] = false; }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int dkj = diag[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const int i = ty + r * TY;\n            const unsigned int v = cur[i][k] + dkj;\n            if (v < cur[i][tx]) {\n                cur[i][tx] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = cur[i][tx];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 3: all remaining local tiles (i, j), i != kb-tile, j != kb, using the\n// local column tile (i, kb) and the pivot panel tile (kb, j). Independent.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase3Kernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n               const unsigned int* __restrict__ panel, const size_t ld,\n               const int kb, const int kbLocal) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int a[TILE][TILE];   // tile (i, kb)\n    __shared__ unsigned int b[TILE][TILE];   // tile (kb, j)\n\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x;\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y;\n    if (bi == kbLocal || bj == kb) return;\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t rowBase = (size_t)bi * TILE * ld;\n    const unsigned int* da = dist + rowBase + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    const unsigned int* db = panel + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + rowBase + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + rowBase + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n\n    unsigned int c[RPT];\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        a[i][tx] = da[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        b[i][tx] = db[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        c[r] = d[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        pk[r] = 0;\n        changed[r] = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 16\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int bkj = b[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const unsigned int v = a[ty + r * TY][k] + bkj;\n            if (v < c[r]) {\n                c[r] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = c[r];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Row-block distribution helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstruct Partition {\n    int nBlocks;        // total number of row blocks (padded n / TILE)\n    size_t nPad;        // padded number of nodes\n    int firstBlock;     // first row block owned by this rank\n    int numBlocks;      // number of row blocks owned by this rank\n};\n\nstatic int blockFirst(const int nBlocks, const int rank, const int size) {\n    return (int)(((long long)nBlocks * rank) / size);\n}\n\nstatic int ownerOfBlock(const int kb, const int nBlocks, const int size) {\n    // Inverse of blockFirst: smallest rank r such that blockFirst(r+1) > kb.\n    int lo = 0, hi = size - 1;\n    while (lo < hi) {\n        const int mid = (lo + hi) / 2;\n        if (blockFirst(nBlocks, mid + 1, size) > kb) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;\n    }\n    return lo;\n}\n\n// Generate the local row band of the distance matrix. The random sequence is\n// identical to the sequential reference (rand_r with seed 42 over the full\n// n*n matrix); only the rows owned by this rank are stored (padded to nPad).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const Partition& part) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(numNodes, rowBegin + (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE);\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n\n    // Padding entries (rows/cols >= numNodes) are unreachable (INF).\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows * nPad; ++i) dist[i] = INF;\n\n    // Sequential stream; skip the entries that belong to other ranks.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBegin * numNodes; ++i) rand_r(&seed);\n    for (size_t r = rowBegin; r < rowEnd; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = dist.data() + (r - rowBegin) * nPad;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < numNodes; ++c) {\n            row[c] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < localRows; ++r) {\n        dist[r * nPad + (rowBegin + r)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Reference semantics: after initialisation path[i][j] == i for all i, j.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const Partition& part) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < localRows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + r * nPad;\n        const unsigned int v = (unsigned int)(rowBegin + r);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < nPad; ++c) row[c] = v;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed blocked Floyd-Warshall. dist/path hold the local row band on the\n// host (localRows x nPad); on return they contain the result for this band.\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const Partition& part, const int rank, const int size) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const int nBlocks = part.nBlocks;\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * nPad;\n    const size_t panelElems = (size_t)TILE * nPad;\n\n    unsigned int* dDist = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPath = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPanel = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* hPanel = nullptr;\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dDist, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPath, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n    }\n    if (size > 1) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n    }\n\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dDist, dist.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dPath, path.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    const dim3 threads(TX, TY);\n\n    for (int kb = 0; kb < nBlocks; ++kb) {\n        const int owner = ownerOfBlock(kb, nBlocks, size);\n        const bool isOwner = (owner == rank);\n        const int kbLocal = isOwner ? (kb - part.firstBlock) : -1;\n        const size_t pivotRowOffset = (size_t)kbLocal * TILE;\n\n        const unsigned int* panel = dPanel;\n\n        if (isOwner) {\n            // Phase 1: diagonal tile\n            fwPhase1Kernel<<<1, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, kb, pivotRowOffset);\n            // Phase 2 (row): pivot row panel\n            fwPhase2RowKernel<<<nBlocks, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, kb, pivotRowOffset);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            panel = dDist + pivotRowOffset * nPad;\n            if (size > 1) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hPanel, panel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (size > 1) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(hPanel, (int)panelElems, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n            if (!isOwner && localElems > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dPanel, hPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (localElems == 0) continue;\n\n        // Phase 2 (column): local tiles in pivot column\n        fwPhase2ColKernel<<<part.numBlocks, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, panel, nPad, kb, kbLocal);\n        // Phase 3: everything else in the local band\n        const dim3 grid3(nBlocks, part.numBlocks);\n        fwPhase3Kernel<<<grid3, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, panel, nPad, kb, kbLocal);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), dDist, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), dPath, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dDist));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPath));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPanel));\n    }\n    if (hPanel) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hPanel));\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed (padded) bands into the full n x n matrix on rank 0.\nvoid gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const Partition& part,\n                          const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size,\n                          std::vector<unsigned int>& full) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(numNodes, rowBegin + (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE);\n    const size_t myRows = rowEnd > rowBegin ? rowEnd - rowBegin : 0;\n\n    // Pack local rows without padding\n    std::vector<unsigned int> packed(myRows * numNodes);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < myRows; ++r) {\n        std::memcpy(packed.data() + r * numNodes, localDist.data() + r * nPad,\n                    numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Transfer in chunks whose element count fits in an int.\n    const size_t rowsPerChunk = std::max<size_t>(1, (size_t)(1u << 30) / std::max<size_t>(1, numNodes));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        full.resize(numNodes * numNodes);\n        std::memcpy(full.data(), packed.data(), packed.size() * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (int src = 1; src < size; ++src) {\n            const int fb = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, src, size);\n            const int nb = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, src + 1, size) - fb;\n            const size_t rb = (size_t)fb * TILE;\n            const size_t re = std::min(numNodes, rb + (size_t)nb * TILE);\n            for (size_t r = rb; r < re; r += rowsPerChunk) {\n                const size_t rows = std::min(rowsPerChunk, re - r);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(full.data() + r * numNodes, (int)(rows * numNodes),\n                                   MPI_UNSIGNED, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < myRows; r += rowsPerChunk) {\n            const size_t rows = std::min(rowsPerChunk, myRows - r);\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(packed.data() + r * numNodes, (int)(rows * numNodes),\n                               MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t lim = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    bool ok = true;\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < lim; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < lim; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n#pragma omp critical\n                        {\n                            printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                                   i, j, k);\n                            ok = false;\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return ok;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int          return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Select one GPU per rank (round-robin over the GPUs visible on this node)\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank));\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));\n        int deviceCount = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n        if printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", size, Each block-round of the blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm\n//             requires one broadcast of the pivot row panel (64 x n) from the\n//             rank that owns it.\n//  * CUDA   : all O(n^3) work is done on the GPU with the classic three-phase\n//             blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm (diagonal tile, pivot row/column\n//             tiles, remaining tiles) using shared-memory tiling and register\n//             blocking.\n//  * OpenMP : host-side O(n^2) work (matrix initialisation / packing, result\n//             gathering, validation) runs multithreaded on each rank.\n//\n// The computed distance matrix is bit-identical to the sequential algorithm\n// (the blocked formulation computes exactly the same shortest-path values).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Blocking parameters for the GPU kernels\nconstexpr int TILE = 64;             // tile edge length (nodes)\nconstexpr int TX = 64;               // threads in x (one per tile column)\nconstexpr int TY = 16;               // threads in y\nconstexpr int RPT = TILE / TY;       // rows per thread (register blocking)\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                              \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                   \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                   \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err__),    \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__);                                              \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                             \\\n        }                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n#define MPI_CHECK(call)                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                              \\\n        int err__ = (call);                                                           \\\n        if (err__ != MPI_SUCCESS) {                                                   \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MPI error %d at %s:%d\\n\", err__, __FILE__, __LINE__);    \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                             \\\n        }                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA kernels\n//\n// All matrices on the device are row-major with leading dimension `ld`\n// (= padded number of nodes). r < RPT; ++r) {\n        s[ty + r * TY][tx] = d[(size_t)(ty + r * TY) * ld + tx];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) { pk[r] = 0; changed[r] = false; }\n\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int skj = s[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const int i = ty + r * TY;\n            const unsigned int v = s[i][k] + skj;\n            if (v < s[i][tx]) {\n                s[i][tx] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = s[i][tx];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 2 (row): tiles (kb, j) for all j != kb, using the finished diagonal tile.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase2RowKernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n                  const size_t ld, const int kb, const size_t tileRowOffset) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int diag[TILE][TILE];\n    __shared__ unsigned int cur[TILE][TILE];\n\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x;\n    if (bj == kb) return;\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const unsigned int* dg = dist + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + tileRowOffset * ld + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = cur[i][tx];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// Phase 3: all remaining local tiles (i, j), i != kb-tile, j != kb, using the\n// local column tile (i, kb) and the pivot panel tile (kb, j). Independent.\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(TX * TY)\nfwPhase3Kernel(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, unsigned int* __restrict__ path,\n               const unsigned int* __restrict__ panel, const size_t ld,\n               const int kb, const int kbLocal) {\n    __shared__ unsigned int a[TILE][TILE];   // tile (i, kb)\n    __shared__ unsigned int b[TILE][TILE];   // tile (kb, j)\n\n    const int bj = blockIdx.x;\n    const int bi = blockIdx.y;\n    if (bi == kbLocal || bj == kb) return;\n\n    const int tx = threadIdx.x;\n    const int ty = threadIdx.y;\n    const size_t rowBase = (size_t)bi * TILE * ld;\n    const unsigned int* da = dist + rowBase + (size_t)kb * TILE;\n    const unsigned int* db = panel + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* d = dist + rowBase + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n    unsigned int* p = path + rowBase + (size_t)bj * TILE;\n\n    unsigned int c[RPT];\n    unsigned int pk[RPT];\n    bool changed[RPT];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        a[i][tx] = da[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        b[i][tx] = db[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        c[r] = d[(size_t)i * ld + tx];\n        pk[r] = 0;\n        changed[r] = false;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 16\n    for (int k = 0; k < TILE; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int bkj = b[k][tx];\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n            const unsigned int v = a[ty + r * TY][k] + bkj;\n            if (v < c[r]) {\n                c[r] = v;\n                pk[r] = kb * TILE + k;\n                changed[r] = true;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int r = 0; r < RPT; ++r) {\n        const int i = ty + r * TY;\n        d[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = c[r];\n        if (changed[r]) p[(size_t)i * ld + tx] = pk[r];\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Row-block distribution helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstruct Partition {\n    int nBlocks;        // total number of row blocks (padded n / TILE)\n    size_t nPad;        // padded number of nodes\n    int firstBlock;     // first row block owned by this rank\n    int numBlocks;      // number of row blocks owned by this rank\n};\n\nstatic int blockFirst(const int nBlocks, const int rank, const int size) {\n    return (int)(((long long)nBlocks * rank) / size);\n}\n\nstatic int ownerOfBlock(const int kb, const int nBlocks, const int size) {\n    // Inverse of blockFirst: smallest rank r such that blockFirst(r+1) > kb.\n    int lo = 0, hi = size - 1;\n    while (lo < hi) {\n        const int mid = (lo + hi) / 2;\n        if (blockFirst(nBlocks, mid + 1, size) > kb) hi = mid; else lo = mid + 1;\n    }\n    return lo;\n}\n\n// Generate the local row band of the distance matrix. The random sequence is\n// identical to the sequential reference (rand_r with seed 42 over the full\n// n*n matrix); only the rows owned by this rank are stored (padded to nPad).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const Partition& part) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(numNodes, rowBegin + (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE);\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n\n    // Padding entries (rows/cols >= numNodes) are unreachable (INF).\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows * nPad; ++i) dist[i] = INF;\n\n    // Sequential stream; skip the entries that belong to other ranks.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rowBegin * numNodes; ++i) rand_r(&seed);\n    for (size_t r = rowBegin; r < rowEnd; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = dist.data() + (r - rowBegin) * nPad;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < numNodes; ++c) {\n            row[c] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < localRows; ++r) {\n        dist[r * nPad + (rowBegin + r)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Reference semantics: after initialisation path[i][j] == i for all i, j.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const Partition& part) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < localRows; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + r * nPad;\n        const unsigned int v = (unsigned int)(rowBegin + r);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < nPad; ++c) row[c] = v;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed blocked Floyd-Warshall. dist/path hold the local row band on the\n// host (localRows x nPad); on return they contain the result for this band.\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const Partition& part, const int rank, const int size) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const int nBlocks = part.nBlocks;\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * nPad;\n    const size_t panelElems = (size_t)TILE * nPad;\n\n    unsigned int* dDist = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPath = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPanel = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* hPanel = nullptr;\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dDist, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPath, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n    }\n    if (size > 1) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n    }\n\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dDist, dist.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dPath, path.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    const dim3 threads(TX, TY);\n\n    for (int kb = 0; kb < nBlocks; ++kb) {\n        const int owner = ownerOfBlock(kb, nBlocks, size);\n        const bool isOwner = (owner == rank);\n        const int kbLocal = isOwner ? (kb - part.firstBlock) : -1;\n        const size_t pivotRowOffset = (size_t)kbLocal * TILE;\n\n        const unsigned int* panel = dPanel;\n\n        if (isOwner) {\n            // Phase 1: diagonal tile\n            fwPhase1Kernel<<<1, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, kb, pivotRowOffset);\n            // Phase 2 (row): pivot row panel\n            fwPhase2RowKernel<<<nBlocks, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, kb, pivotRowOffset);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            panel = dDist + pivotRowOffset * nPad;\n            if (size > 1) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hPanel, panel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (size > 1) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(hPanel, (int)panelElems, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n            if (!isOwner && localElems > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dPanel, hPanel, panelElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (localElems == 0) continue;\n\n        // Phase 2 (column): local tiles in pivot column\n        fwPhase2ColKernel<<<part.numBlocks, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, panel, nPad, kb, kbLocal);\n        // Phase 3: everything else in the local band\n        const dim3 grid3(nBlocks, part.numBlocks);\n        fwPhase3Kernel<<<grid3, threads>>>(dDist, dPath, panel, nPad, kb, kbLocal);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    }\n\n    if (localElems > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dist.data(), dDist, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(path.data(), dPath, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dDist));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPath));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPanel));\n    }\n    if (hPanel) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(hPanel));\n}\n\n// Gather the distributed (padded) bands into the full n x n matrix on rank 0.\nvoid gatherDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const Partition& part,\n                          const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size,\n                          std::vector<unsigned int>& full) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const size_t rowBegin = (size_t)part.firstBlock * TILE;\n    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(numNodes, rowBegin + (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE);\n    const size_t myRows = rowEnd > rowBegin ? rowEnd - rowBegin : 0;\n\n    // Pack local rows without padding\n    std::vector<unsigned int> packed(myRows * numNodes);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < myRows; ++r) {\n        std::memcpy(packed.data() + r * numNodes, localDist.data() + r * nPad,\n                    numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n    }\n\n    // Transfer in chunks whose element count fits in an int.\n    const size_t rowsPerChunk = std::max<size_t>(1, (size_t)(1u << 30) / std::max<size_t>(1, numNodes));\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        full.resize(numNodes * numNodes);\n        std::memcpy(full.data(), packed.data(), packed.size() * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        for (int src = 1; src < size; ++src) {\n            const int fb = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, src, size);\n            const int nb = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, src + 1, size) - fb;\n            const size_t rb = (size_t)fb * TILE;\n            const size_t re = std::min(numNodes, rb + (size_t)nb * TILE);\n            for (size_t r = rb; r < re; r += rowsPerChunk) {\n                const size_t rows = std::min(rowsPerChunk, re - r);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Recv(full.data() + r * numNodes, (int)(rows * numNodes),\n                                   MPI_UNSIGNED, src, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < myRows; r += rowsPerChunk) {\n            const size_t rows = std::min(rowsPerChunk, myRows - r);\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Send(packed.data() + r * numNodes, (int)(rows * numNodes),\n                               MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    const size_t lim = std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10));\n    bool ok = true;\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < lim; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < lim; ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n#pragma omp critical\n                        {\n                            printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                                   i, j, k);\n                            ok = false;\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return ok;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Init(&argc, &argv));\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank));\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size));\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Select one GPU per rank (round-robin over the GPUs visible on this node)\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank,\n                                      MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm));\n        int localRank = 0;\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank));\n        MPI_CHECK(MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm));\n        int deviceCount = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n        if (deviceCount < 1) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices found\\n\");\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));  // establish context now, outside the timed region\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\\n\", size, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // Row-block partition\n    Partition part;\n    part.nBlocks = (int)((numNodes + TILE - 1) / TILE);\n    part.nPad = (size_t)part.nBlocks * TILE;\n    part.firstBlock = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, rank, size);\n    part.numBlocks = blockFirst(part.nBlocks, rank + 1, size) - part.firstBlock;\n    const size_t localElems = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE * part.nPad;\n\n    // Allocate matrices (local row band)\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localElems);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localElems);\n\n    // Initialize\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE, part);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, part);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, part, rank, size);\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<unsigned int> full;\n        gatherDistanceMatrix(dist, part, numNodes, rank, size, full);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n            if (printResults) {\n                print_results_int(full, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n            }\n\n            // Validation\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                bool valid = validateResult(full, numNodes);\n\n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":3,"newStart":1,"newLines":22,"lines":["+// Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark","+//","+// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation.","+//","+//  * MPI    : the distance matrix is distributed by contiguous blocks of rows","+//             (64-row block granularity) across ranks; every rank drives one","+//             GPU. blocked Floyd-Warshall. dist/path hold the local row band on the\n// host (localRows x nPad); on return they contain the result for this band.\n//\n// Lookahead pipeline: in round kb the owner of pivot block kb+1 first brings\n// its row block kb+1 up to date, computes the next pivot panel (phases 1 and 2\n// row) and starts its broadcast; the broadcast then overlaps with phase 3 of\n// round kb on all ranks.\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const Partition& part, const int rank, const int size) {\n    const size_t nPad = part.nPad;\n    const int nBlocks = part.nBlocks;\n    const size_t localRows = (size_t)part.numBlocks * TILE;\n    const size_t localElems = localRows * nPad;\n    const int* dDist = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPath = nullptr;\n    unsigned int* dPanel[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    unsigned int* hPanel[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    cudaStream_t compute, copy;\n    cudaEvent_t evD2H[2], evH2D[2];\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&copy, cudaStreamNonBlocking));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evD2H[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreateWithFlags(&evH2D[b], cudaEventDisableTiming));\n    }\n\n    if (haveRows) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dDist, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPath, localElems * sizeof(unsigned int)));\n        if (multi) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPanel[0], panelBytes));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPanel[1], panelBytes));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dDist, dist.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dPath, path.data(), localElems * sizeof(unsigned int),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));\n    }\n    if (multi) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hPanel[0], panelBytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hPanel[1], panelBytes));\n    }\n\n    const dim3 threads(TX, TY);\n    MPI_Request req = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n    auto localIndex = [&](const int kb) {\n        return (kb >= part.firstBlock && kb < part.firstBlock + part.numBlocks)\n                   ? kb - part.firstBlock : -1;\n    };\n\n    // Pivot panel for round 0\n    {\n        const int owner = ownerOfBlock(0, nBlocks, size);\n        if (owner == rank) {\n            fwPhase1Kernel<<<1, threads, 0, compute>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, 0, 0);\n            fwPhase2RowKernel<<<nBlocks, threads, 0, compute>>>(dDist, dPath, nPad, 0, 0);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n            if (multi) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(hPanel[0], dDist, panelBytes,\n                                           cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, compute));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));\n            }\n        }\n        if (multi) {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Ibcast(hPanel[0], (int)panelElems, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner,\n                                 MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req));\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kb = 0; kb < nBlocks; ++kb) {\n        const int buf = kb & 1;\n available on the device\n        const unsigned int* panel = nullptr;\n        if (isOwner) {\n            if (multi) MPI_CHECK(MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n            panel = dDist + (size_t)kbLocal * TILE * nPad;\n        } else {\n            MPI_CHECK(MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE));\n            if (haveRows) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dPanel[buf], hPanel[buf], panelBytes,\n                                           cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, copy));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(evH2D[buf], copy));\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(compute, evH2D[buf], 0));\n            }\n            panel = dPanel[buf];\n        }\n\n        if (!haveRows) {\n            // Nothing to compute, but keep participating in the broadcasts\n            if (multi && kb + 1 < nBlocks) {\n                const int nextOwner = ownerOfBlock(kb + 1, nBlocks, size);\n                MPI_CHECK(MPI_Ibcast(hPanel[buf ^ 1], (int)panelElems, MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                                     nextOwner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req));\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011CgNCC3KAzawkLL3Xv5y3k","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Everything is verified. Here is the recap.\n\n**What I did**\n\nThe benchmark in `floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp` is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, built from the same single source file and producing the same `floydwarshall` executable. No new files were created.\n\n- **CUDA** does all O(n³) work with the three-phase blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm on 64x64 tiles. Phase 3, which dominates, uses shared-memory tiling with 4x2 register blocking per thread. The matrix is padded to a multiple of 64 with unreachable entries, so arbitrary n works.\n- **MPI** distributes contiguous 64-row blocks across ranks, one GPU per rank (chosen by node-local rank). Each round, the owner of the next pivot block computes it first, stages it to pinned host memory on a side stream, and sends it with non-blocking point-to-point messages to every rank that owns rows. That transfer overlaps with phase 3 on all GPUs. Panels are double-buffered on host and device, with CUDA events guarding buffer reuse.\n- **OpenMP** handles the host-side O(n²) work on each rank: band initialisation, path initialisation, result packing for the gather, and the sampled triangle-inequality validation.\n- **CMakeLists.txt** enables the CUDA language, compiles the .cpp as CUDA via a source property, and links MPI, OpenMP, and the math library. CUDA architectures default to the GPUs present at configure time.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nThe distance matrix hash matches the original sequential program bit-for-bit for n = 100, 512, 1000, and 4096 with 1 through 5 ranks, including runs where some ranks own no rows. At n = 8192 and 16384 the hash is identical across 1 to 4 ranks. Validation passes everywhere. The path matrix is still maintained but, as in the original, is never output. Because the blocked algorithm visits pivots in a different order, its recorded intermediate node for a given pair may differ from the sequential code's choice, though it is still a valid one.\n\n**Performance on this machine (4x RTX 3090, timed region includes device transfers and all communication)**\n\n| n | sequential | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 4096 | ~70 s | 70 ms | ~50 ms | ~80 ms |\n| 8192 | not run | 380 ms | 213 ms | 140 ms |\n| 16384 | not run | 2651 ms | 1479 ms | 794 to 847 ms |\n\nTwo problems I found and fixed along the way: the MPI library has no asynchronous progress, so sends stalled while the host blocked on a CUDA event. The event wait now polls and drives MPI progress. I also caught a buffer-reuse race across the ownership boundary and ordered the panel uploads behind the previous round's kernels.\n\n**Caveats**\n\n- The random initialisation must replay the original sequential rand_r stream, so each rank skips ahead serially. That is O(n²) and outside the timed region, but noticeable above n ≈ 20000.\n- Small sizes (n ≤ 4096) do not benefit from more than 2 GPUs. The per-round pivot chain and panel transfer dominate there.\n- The build needs CMake 3.18 or newer, which was already available."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":32,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2556,"cache_read_input_tokens":96677,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2556},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"384b6de8-8353-4e07-a418-b346fbed3c68","uuid":"35cded28-57b4-4f1c-a9e6-1284c7e8036d","timestamp":"2026-10-25T02:49:27.366Z","request_id":"req_011CgNCC36Wz4XKUd5b34ruF"}

Here is the recap.\n\n**What I did**\n\nThe benchmark in `floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp` is now a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA program, built from the same single source file and producing the same `floydwarshall` executable. No new files were created.\n\n- **CUDA** does all O(n³) work with the three-phase blocked Floyd-Warshall algorithm on 64x64 tiles. Phase 3, which dominates, uses shared-memory tiling with 4x2 register blocking per thread. The matrix is padded to a multiple of 64 with unreachable entries, so arbitrary n works.\n- **MPI** distributes contiguous 64-row blocks across ranks, one GPU per rank (chosen by node-local rank). Each round, the owner of the next pivot block computes it first, stages it to pinned host memory on a side stream, and sends it with non-blocking point-to-point messages to every rank that owns rows. That transfer overlaps with phase 3 on all GPUs. Panels are double-buffered on host and device, with CUDA events guarding buffer reuse.\n- **OpenMP** handles the host-side O(n²) work on each rank: band initialisation, path initialisation, result packing for the gather, and the sampled triangle-inequality validation.\n- **CMakeLists.txt** enables the CUDA language, compiles the .cpp as CUDA via a source property, and links MPI, OpenMP, and the math library. CUDA architectures default to the GPUs present at configure time.\n\n**Correctness**\n\nThe distance matrix hash matches the original sequential program bit-for-bit for n = 100, 512, 1000, and 4096 with 1 through 5 ranks, including runs where some ranks own no rows. At n = 8192 and 16384 the hash is identical across 1 to 4 ranks. Validation passes everywhere. The path matrix is still maintained but, as in the original, is never output. Because the blocked algorithm visits pivots in a different order, its recorded intermediate node for a given pair may differ from the sequential code's choice, though it is still a valid one.\n\n**Performance on this machine (4x RTX 3090, timed region includes device transfers and all communication)**\n\n| n | sequential | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 4096 | ~70 s | 70 ms | ~50 ms | ~80 ms |\n| 8192 | not run | 380 ms | 213 ms | 140 ms |\n| 16384 | not run | 2651 ms | 1479 ms | 794 to 847 ms |\n\nTwo problems I found and fixed along the way: the MPI library has no asynchronous progress, so sends stalled while the host blocked on a CUDA event. The event wait now polls and drives MPI progress. I also caught a buffer-reuse race across the ownership boundary and ordered the panel uploads behind the previous round's kernels.\n\n**Caveats**\n\n- The random initialisation must replay the original sequential rand_r stream, so each rank skips ahead serially. That is O(n²) and outside the timed region, but noticeable above n ≈ 20000.\n- Small sizes (n ≤ 4096) do not benefit from more than 2 GPUs.
